# John Galt (@AtlasShrug1) posts on X, 2025-07-31 to 2026-11-08

**Speakers:** John Galt (@AtlasShrug1 on X; anonymous trader of AI, semiconductor and critical-mineral stocks who reads the cycle from market tickers)

**Summary:** 1,359 posts, Jul 2025 to 8 Nov 2026. Mostly single-stock trading; from May 2026 a sustained late-cycle call: AI/semis bubble top, dollar squeeze to DXY 108-110 and growth scare, then QE and a hard-asset reflation. Method: tells from homebuilders, financials, staples, breadth, oil, the dollar, Korea (EWY) and the tape's reaction to good news; 85-94% cash, hedged, never net short.

**Approach:** discretionary cycle reading, market internals, dollar and liquidity, sector rotation, AI and semiconductor equities, short selling and hedging
